### Fixed (reverse-silo)
- **`scripts/post-new.ts`**: bug preexistente latente (`??` mezclado con `||` sin parentesis = SyntaxError). El scaffold estaba roto; corregido con los parentesis correctos.

### Fixed (sitemap-freshness)
- **`page-sitemap.xml` dejó de emitir un timestamp de build como `<lastmod>`**: el endpoint estampaba `new Date().toISOString()` en las 14 rutas estáticas y en los 5 paquetes, así que cada página figuraba modificada "hoy" en cada deploy. Eso es exactamente lo que la nueva regla de frescura (AGENTS.md §11) prohíbe: afirmar que todo cambió siempre le enseña a Google a ignorar el campo. Ahora la fecha vive **al lado del contenido**: un `meta.ts` con `contentUpdated` colocado junto a cada ruta (`src/routes/(public)/<ruta>/meta.ts`), recolectado vía `import.meta.glob` en `src/lib/utils/sitemap.ts`, y un campo `updated` (validado por Zod) por paquete en `src/lib/data/packages.ts`.
- **Fechas semilla honestas (verificadas en el historial)**: las fechas se fijaron al último commit que cambió el CONTENIDO real, no el que agregó el campo. Los 5 paquetes = `2026-05-31` (commit `f88e8ff`, filtros e-commerce; el commit de precios `1d74bb0` del 2026-07-23 solo agregó la API derivada, +84 líneas, sin tocar ningún dato de paquete). Home = `2026-06-16`, about-us y sitemap = `2026-06-14` (su último cambio de contenido antes del refactor de precios, que no alteró lo renderizado).
- **Gotcha registrado (`import.meta.glob` + route groups)**: un patrón con el grupo `(public)` en el glob (`/src/routes/(public)/**/meta.ts`) NO matchea nada - picomatch interpreta los paréntesis como un grupo extglob. Se globea `/src/routes/**/meta.ts` y se acepta SOLO lo que esté bajo `/(public)/` (vía `indexOf`), ignorando cualquier `meta.ts` de otro grupo en vez de mapearlo a una clave espuria. El mapa se computa una sola vez al cargar el módulo.

### Added (sitemap-freshness)
- **`src/lib/utils/sitemap.ts`**: helper único. `toLastmod()` (antes copiado idéntico en `post/category/author-sitemap`), `STATIC_SITEMAP_PAGES` (registro de rutas del sitemap) y `getStaticPageFreshness()` (mapa derivado, memoizado a nivel de módulo).
- **`src/lib/data/sitemap-freshness.test.ts`** (vitest): guard que falla la suite si una ruta de `STATIC_SITEMAP_PAGES` no tiene su `meta.ts`, si un paquete no declara `updated`, si el formato no es `YYYY-MM-DD`, o si alguna fecha está en el futuro (un timestamp de build lo estaría). La solución siempre es declarar la fecha, nunca ampliar un allowlist.
- **`tests/sitemaps.spec.ts`** (Playwright, +1 spec): pone la invariante de frescura también en el gate E2E (no solo en vitest): afirma que NINGUNA `<url>` viaja sin `<lastmod>`, que las fechas de paquete se DERIVAN de `packages[].updated` (no hardcodeadas), que aparece más de un valor distinto (el bug estampaba el mismo para todos) y que ninguna URL declara una fecha futura. Suite completa en verde (134/134).

### Changed (docs-seo-agents)
- **`AGENTS.md` listo para producción con los agentes de SEO/Local-SEO globales**: nueva sección "SEO y Contenido: agentes globales" (dónde viven los agentes `~/.claude/agents/`, contexto global vs `.agents/context/` por proyecto, orden de operaciones con requisitos duros, E-E-A-T leyendo el PDF de Google, cuándo NO usarlos) y "Lo que este proyecto declara" (build real y salida en `.svelte-kit/cloudflare/`, tabla de fuentes únicas de verdad, suite de tests, NAP exacto y GBP). Precedencia ante conflicto declarada: **PDF de Google > AGENTS.md > SEO.md**. Se añadieron las reglas §11 (frescura de fechas) y §12 (sin caracteres tipográficos de IA, aclarando que tildes y ñ son idioma y se mantienen), y se limpiaron las rayas y puntos suspensivos que el propio documento incumplía.
- **`SEO.md`**: corregida la referencia a archivos `.mdx` del blog (este proyecto usa `.svx`/mdsvex) y limpiadas sus semirrayas.

### Fixed (e2e-suite-green)
- **Suite E2E de vuelta en verde (133/133)**: 20 specs llevaban tiempo en rojo. Ninguna señalaba un bug de producción - el código estaba bien y los tests habían quedado atrás de dos refactors deliberados. Se corrigieron los tests, no el código.
- **Aserciones sobre el grafo de entidades JSON-LD (9 specs)**: tras los commits `865df66` (Service `provider` por `@id`) y `5698ed1` (Article `publisher` por `@id`), la empresa se referencia por `@id` al nodo canónico `#organization` en vez de redefinirse inline - precisamente para eliminar el `LocalBusiness` duplicado e incompleto que GSC marcaba. Los tests seguían leyendo `service.provider.name` y `article.publisher.url`, siempre `undefined` desde entonces. Ahora verifican la referencia real (`provider['@id']` / `publisher['@id']` === `https://malagaeventgear.com/#organization`), que es la aserción con valor: comprueba que el grafo apunta al nodo único. Además: el `ItemList` de `/packages/` envuelve cada paquete en un `ListItem.item` con su entidad `Service`+`Offer` desde `f1eb723`, así que el test lee `.item.name` / `.item.url` y de paso valida `@type`, `offers.price` y `priceCurrency`; y el `ContactPage.url` se espera con trailing slash, coherente con `trailingSlash: 'always'` (AGENTS.md §2).
- **Contenido diferido por IntersectionObserver (11 specs)**: los marquees de imágenes (`ImageMarquee.svelte`) y el bloque de testimonios (`LazyMount.svelte`) montan su contenido sólo cuando el placeholder se acerca al viewport - una optimización de LCP deliberada y documentada en ambos componentes. Los tests afirmaban sobre `[data-testid="testimonials"]` y sobre los `<img>` del marquee **sin scrollear**, de modo que el observer nunca disparaba y el contenido no existía en el DOM. Se añadió el helper `tests/support/lazy.ts` (`revealLazyContent`), que recorre la página en pasos de una pantalla para disparar todos los observers y vuelve al top. **Se arregló el test, no el componente**: hacer que el contenido renderice de forma eager habría deshecho la optimización de LCP.
  - Gotcha registrado: el reveal debe ir **después** de esperar la hidratación (`waitForLoadState`). El observer se arma en `onMount`; scrollear antes de que el cliente hidrate no dispara nada, y el contenedor (SSR) se ve visible mientras su track de imágenes sigue vacío - lo que despista el diagnóstico.
- **Gotcha de `playwright.config.ts` (`reuseExistingServer: !CI`)**: fuera de CI, Playwright reutiliza cualquier dev server que ya escuche en :5173. Comparar ramas o hacer `git stash` para aislar una regresión da resultados **inválidos** si queda un server viejo levantado, porque los tests siguen pegándole al módulo cacheado. Hay que matar el proceso de :5173 antes de una corrida de referencia.

### Changed (packages-single-source-of-truth)
- **Capa de precios derivada en `packages.ts`**: El SSoT de paquetes ya existía, pero solo guardaba el número - cada consumidor formateaba el precio a mano, y el catálogo se había filtrado a 5 sitios más. Se añadió la API derivada: `CURRENCY`, `CURRENCY_SYMBOL`, `VAT_RATE`, `formatPrice(amount, lang)` (respeta la convención de cada idioma: `€290` en inglés, `290 €` en español), `getPriceRange()`, `formatPriceRange(lang)`, `getSchemaPriceRange()`, `getPackageLabels(lang)`, `getHomepageShowcasePackages()`. Nuevo campo `navIcon` en `PackageSchema` para que los listados de navegación tampoco necesiten un mapa paralelo de iconos.
- **Eliminada la duplicación en 6 consumidores**:
  - `src/routes/(public)/sitemap/+page.svelte`: los 5 packs con su precio estaban escritos a mano **dos veces** (bloque EN y bloque ES) - 10 literales. Ahora un `{#each}` sobre el catálogo.
  - `src/lib/utils/schema.ts` + `src/routes/(public)/about-us/+page.svelte`: **el sitio publicaba dos `priceRange` distintos para el mismo `@id` `#organization`** - el vago `'€€'` desde el layout y `'290€ - 650€'` desde `/about-us/`. Peor: el nodo duplicado de about-us traía su propia dirección (`'Avenida de Barcelona, 34'`), inconsistente con el NAP de `site.ts` (`'Av. de Barcelona, 34, Distrito Centro'`), más su propio teléfono, email y horario. Se eliminó el nodo duplicado (`AboutPage` ahora referencia `#organization` y `#website` **por `@id`**, siguiendo la convención ya documentada en `buildServiceSchema`) y `buildLocalBusinessSchema()` deriva el `priceRange` real del catálogo.
  - `src/lib/data/faq.ts`: la respuesta "paquetes populares" listaba los 5 packs con precio en EN y ES. Se genera con `packagesWithPrices(lang)`. Relevante porque esta FAQ **se publica como JSON-LD `FAQPage`**: un precio viejo ahí llegaba a los resultados enriquecidos de Google.
  - `src/lib/i18n.svelte.ts`: el bloque `packages` duplicaba nombres, precios, descripciones y features de 3 packs en ambos idiomas (`ecoPrice: '€290'`, `weddingTitle`, `ecoF1`...). **Era código muerto** - nadie las consumía, la home ya derivaba de `packages.ts` - y ya divergía del catálogo (no existían claves para Product Presentation ni Basic MICE). Eliminadas; sobreviven solo `badge`, `title`, `subtitle` y `enquire`, que sí son copy genérico.
  - `src/routes/(public)/+page.svelte` y `src/routes/(public)/llms.txt/+server.ts`: ambos calculaban `Math.min(...)` / `Math.max(...)` sobre los precios por su cuenta; ahora usan `getPriceRange()`. El `21% VAT` de llms.txt se deriva de `VAT_RATE`.

### Added (packages-single-source-of-truth)
- **`src/lib/data/packages-pricing.test.ts`**: 20 tests de contrato de la API de precios. Afirman **comportamiento** (el mínimo es el pack más barato, el español pospone el símbolo, `route` concuerda con `slug`, todo pack tiene `navIcon`), no importes literales - así un cambio legítimo de tarifa no los rompe, solo los rompe una derivación mal hecha.
- **`src/lib/data/no-hardcoded-prices.test.ts`**: guard que escanea todo `src/**` vía `import.meta.glob` y **falla la suite** ante cualquier literal `€290` / `290 €` / `290 EUR` fuera del allowlist. Detectó 4 ficheros que la revisión manual había pasado por alto. Incluye un meta-test (`scans a meaningful number of files`) para que un glob roto no lo convierta en un no-op silencioso, y otro que verifica que el patrón realmente detecta violaciones sin dispararse con `290px`. Usa `import.meta.glob` y no `node:fs` porque `tsconfig.types` está restringido a las definiciones de Cloudflare Workers a propósito (y AGENTS.md §3 ya manda leer ficheros así).
- **`tests/pricing-consistency.spec.ts`**: 6 specs E2E que prueban la otra mitad - que lo **renderizado** concuerda con el catálogo: el sitemap HTML lista cada pack con su precio, el `priceRange` del JSON-LD es el derivado en `/`, `/about-us/` y `/packages/`, `/about-us/` **no** redefine `#organization` (se afirma que existe exactamente 1 definición), la respuesta FAQ nombra cada pack a su precio (verificado contra el JSON-LD `FAQPage`, no contra el acordeón colapsado), y `/llms.txt` concuerda con la página `/packages/`.

### Added (llms-txt-geo)
- **`/llms.txt` GEO Endpoint**: Added `src/routes/(public)/llms.txt/+server.ts`, a prerendered `text/plain` endpoint implementing the [llms.txt standard](https://llmstxt.org) (Jeremy Howard, 2024) so AI assistants (ChatGPT, Perplexity, Claude, Gemini) read an authoritative summary of MEG instead of inferring one by crawling. Emits `# H1` + `>` summary + the sections `Docs`, `Packages`, `Blog`, `Key Facts`, `Contact`, `Legal`, `Optional`. **Every fact is derived at build time, never hardcoded** (AGENTS.md §7): packages, prices, guest limits and inclusions come from `$lib/data/packages`; NAP, emails, hours, business categories and the 23 service areas from `$lib/data/site`; the published-post count and the full live category list from `getPosts()` / `getCategories()` in `$lib/data/blog`. Superseded the initial `static/llms.txt` draft, which hardcoded the same data and had already drifted (it was missing the live `/blog/category/gadgets/` page). Only the per-category prose blurbs are literal, with a name-based fallback so a newly added category can never produce a missing entry.
- **`tests/llms-txt.spec.ts` E2E Suite**: Six Playwright specs (AGENTS.md §8) asserting the endpoint returns 200 `text/plain`, keeps the spec's required structure, and - critically - that **every price, capacity and route in the output equals the value imported from `packages.ts`**, so a package edit that does not reach `/llms.txt` fails CI instead of silently quoting customers an obsolete price. Also asserts every live category from `category-sitemap.xml` is present, that NAP matches `siteConfig`, that all link targets are absolute with trailing slashes, and that `/llms.txt` is referenced in `robots.txt` while staying out of the XML sitemaps.

### Changed (repo-hygiene)
- **`robots.txt` references `/llms.txt`**: Documented the file's location and recorded the deliberate decision to keep it **out of** `page-sitemap.xml`. XML sitemaps declare indexable HTML content; `/llms.txt` is a machine-guidance resource fetched by well-known path, and listing it would only invite Google to index a plaintext file we do not want ranking.
- **Narrowed the `.claude/` git ignore**: The previous blanket `.claude/` rule (commit `1785168`) also untracked `.claude/commands/test-lighthouse.md`, removing the `/test-lighthouse` slash command from every fresh clone even though AGENTS.md §10 still mandates that workflow and Claude Code only loads commands from `.claude/` (the `.agents/commands/` copy is documentation, not a working mirror). Replaced with `.claude/*` + `!.claude/commands/`, so the shared commands stay tracked while `.claude/settings.json` - 159 lines of per-machine permission allowlist containing absolute `/Users/...` paths and temp-dir globs - correctly stays untracked. The rule also got its own comment block; it was previously filed under the unrelated "re-creatable via skills-lock.json" comment.
- **Ignored `.agents/context/`**: Scraped third-party research context with no consumer in the codebase, including a 4.1 MB PNG screenshot that a `git add .` would have committed into history permanently.

### Fixed
- **Homepage PageSpeed `NO_LCP` (mobile)**: Resolved the hard `NO_LCP` error (LCP/TBT `Error!`) that collapsed the home's Performance score. Root cause: the home had no real above-the-fold `<img>`; its full-viewport hero backdrop (image/gradient) is excluded from LCP candidacy by PageSpeed's Chrome while still holding the "largest paint" slot, suppressing every smaller candidate → zero LCP candidates. Fix: render the hero photo as a **real, bounded (`aspect-4/3 object-cover`, not full-viewport), `eager` + `fetchpriority="high"`, above-the-fold `<img>`** (the same pattern that makes `/packages/` score 93), replacing the 3 hero bento cards. Added center-cropped 4:3 webp variants (`static/hero-stage.webp` 1024×768, `static/hero-stage-mobile.webp` 700×525) with `<picture>` + preloads, and prerendered the home (`(public)/+page.ts` → `prerender = true`) to lower TTFB. Home went from always-`NO_LCP` to ~93. **NOT caused by Cloudflare Bot Fight Mode** (it was OFF; the `*.workers.dev` URL without the edge also returned `NO_LCP`) - full writeup in `docs/home-no-lcp-investigation.md`. Residual PSI run-to-run intermittency is lab variance on a borderline LCP, not a regression.
- **Normalized Legacy Links in Blog Posts**: Normalized 265 absolute and outdated relative internal links (e.g. pointing to legacy paths `/wedding-pack/`, `/pricing/`, `/contact-us/`, etc.) across all 75+ blog post `.svx` files, using robust regex patterns to avoid partial-path overrides, directing them to their new SvelteKit canonical trailing-slash targets (e.g., `/packages/wedding/`, `/packages/`, `/contact/`). Validated that local production builds compile cleanly with zero 404 errors.
- **Svelte-Check Type-Checking wrangler types**: Created a helper script (`scripts/fix-types.ts`) integrated into `package.json` (`build`, `check`, `gen`) to patch the auto-generated `worker-configuration.d.ts` file, replacing `typeof import("./.svelte-kit/cloudflare/_worker")` with `any`. This prevents TypeScript and `svelte-check` from type-checking the minified Edge worker JS bundle, fixing 1098 compiler errors.
- **Playwright Copy Link Tooltip Click Gesture**: Updated the "Copy Link" desktop E2E test in `tests/share.spec.ts` to trigger a native Playwright `.click()` instead of a synthetic `dispatchEvent('click')`. This ensures a true user gesture is simulated, allowing the secure Clipboard API to resolve and set the tooltip visible under parallel test runners.
- **FAQ Page Reveal Animation on Filter Switch**: Resolved a bug where switching categories on the FAQ page left items with 0 opacity by exposing the layout's scroll reveal `scan` method via Svelte Context and calling it within an `$effect` block on filter state changes.
- **Scroll Toggles for Share FAB and Sticky Sidebar**: Fixed a race condition where the left sidebar share block and mobile FAB displayed on page load or near the top of the scroll viewport, using reactive derivation (`isTopVisible = $derived(isTopShareIntersecting || isNearTop)`).
- **CSS Typo in Share Drawer Buttons**: Fixed CSS layout bug by renaming invalid property `grid-template-cols` to `grid-template-columns` in `ShareThis.svelte`.
- **A11y Compiler Warning Silencing**: Silenced Svelte 5 accessibility compiler warnings in `ShareThis.svelte` and `ClickToTweet.svelte` by adding targeted compiler ignores for element interactions.
- **MICE Package Page Timeout**: Fixed intermittent hydration timeouts on package-page E2E testing by changing the load wait state from `'networkidle'` to `'load'` and using web-first regex `toHaveText(/verificar/i)` assertions.
- **Type Casts in CRM test suites**: Added `unknown` intermediate casting for `mockEnv` object manipulations in `service.test.ts` to satisfy strict TypeScript object index rules.

### Changed
- **Trailing Slash Targets in _redirects**: Aligned Cloudflare Pages redirects in `_redirects` to point directly to SvelteKit's canonical trailing-slash targets, avoiding double redirection hops (TTFB optimization).
- **Professional Package Names in Emails**: Mapped raw package IDs (like `presentation` or `basic-mice`) inside confirmation and internal notification emails to their capitalized professional names (e.g., `Product Presentation Pack`, `Basic MICE Pack`) resolving from the centralized `packages.ts` store, including a localized fallback for general inquiries.
- **Canonical Domain to malagaeventgear.com (no-www)**: Updated OpenGraph and Schema E2E assertions in `tests/opengraph.spec.ts` and `tests/schema.spec.ts` to expect the canonical domain without `www.` (`https://malagaeventgear.com`).

Integrated the high-performance scrolling `ImageMarquee` component and verified client `Testimonials` (Google My Business reviews) below the profile cards on the `/meet-the-team/` page to drive brand validation and trust. - **Bilingual Structured Data & FAQ Schemas**: Added dynamic locale support (`i18n.lang`) to the homepage `FAQPage` schema JSON-LD injection, preventing language mismatch flags in Google Rich Results. Integrated dynamic `FAQPage` schema injection for package-specific FAQs on all individual `/packages/[slug]/` landing pages.

Integrated a visual 4-step process timeline explaining the "delivery-only" full setup and teardown workflow. Added collapsible localized FAQs to address common customer objections (setup, areas, rain policy, technician support). Included 3-bullet core benefits in the Hero section, and updated the LeadForm with high-trust micro-copy (no credit card required, response as soon as possible) and conversion-oriented titles and button labels. Refactored the corresponding E2E tests in `tests/e2e/package-page.spec.ts`.

Integrated `cover-thumbs.json` lookup mapping to serve responsive crop sizes (`srcset`) and thumbnails (`src`) directly from Malaga Event Gear's Cloudflare R2 CDN, maximizing mobile and desktop performance without large local binaries.

### Fixed (blog-table-overlap)
- **Table Overlap & Responsive Scroll**: Added the `min-w-0` class to the `<article>` element (the center grid item in the blog layout) in `src/lib/layouts/BlogPost.svelte` to prevent CSS Grid column expansion beyond the viewport container. Standardized `.table-wrap` styling with `width: 100%` and `max-width: 100%` to ensure that wide tables trigger the responsive horizontal scroll instead of overlapping the Table of Contents (ToC) sidebar.

### Added (blog-image-marquees)
- **Content-Relevant Blog Image Marquees**: Built an automated TypeScript script (`scripts/inject-blog-marquees.ts`) that parses and classifies all 75+ Svelte mdsvex blog posts (`.svx`) into `wedding`, `mice` (corporate), or `eco` (general) packages based on frontmatter categories and slugs, injecting Svelte imports and `<ImageMarquee>` components at a target H2 heading around 60% of the content depth.

### Added (click-to-tweet)
- **Click to Tweet Text Selection** (`src/lib/components/blog/ClickToTweet.svelte`): A premium Svelte 5 component that monitors selection events within the blog post body (`.prose` container), displaying a glassmorphic floating tooltip above the text range allowing users to tweet selections (`"Selected text" - URL`) via X's Web Intent.
- **BlogPost Layout Integration** (`src/lib/layouts/BlogPost.svelte`): Mounted the `<ClickToTweet>` component dynamically inside the blog layout, passing the post's canonical URL and title.
- **E2E Playwright Tests** (`tests/click-to-tweet.spec.ts`): Built test suite validating tooltip visibility, range limits (3-200 chars), page boundary checks (no tooltips outside `.prose`), and successful popup redirect interception.

### Added (share-this-component)
- **ShareThis Component** (`src/lib/components/blog/ShareThis.svelte`): Svelte 5 component supporting three flexible modes: `inline` (horizontal share bar), `sidebar` (vertical sticky share bar), and `drawer` (mobile floating FAB that opens a sliding bottom-sheet with a glassmorphic backdrop filter).
- **Mobile Overlap Prevention**: Standardized bottom coordinate for Share FAB (`bottom: calc(7.25rem + env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px))`) and modified the WhatsApp widget (`WhatsAppWidget.svelte` with `style="bottom: calc(1.5rem + env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px))"`) to stack cleanly above WhatsApp in mobile screen orientations and dynamically handle iOS safe-area home indicators.
- **Icon Standardization**: Cleaned up the mobile Share FAB custom SVG with explicit SVG stroke/fill properties on child nodes to ensure rendering consistency on mobile browsers and matching the share-plus styling.
- **Package Details Integration** (`packages/[slug]/+page.svelte`): Deployed the ShareThis component (both inline and mobile FAB drawer) to the service package landing pages with Intersection Observer scroll tracking.
- **Share Links Utility** (`src/lib/utils/share.ts`): Decoupled pure functions to generate correctly encoded sharing URLs for WhatsApp, Facebook, Twitter, Pinterest, LinkedIn, and Email, as well as clipboard copying helpers.
- **Scroll-driven Visibility Toggle**: Integrated Intersection Observer inside `BlogPost.svelte` and `+page.svelte` layouts to track top inline share widget visibility, dynamically fading in the left sticky sidebar share widget on desktop and the mobile FAB on mobile when the top widget scroll pasts.
- **E2E and Unit Tests**: Added Vitest unit tests (`src/lib/utils/share.test.ts`) for share URL generation, and Playwright integration tests (`tests/share.spec.ts`) validating desktop scroll visibility toggling, mobile FAB/drawer click transitions, copy-link permissions and tooltips, and package landing page sharing.



### Added (wp-blog-migration)
- **Native mdsvex Blog System**: Implemented a full native blog replacing the WordPress placeholder page. Posts live in `src/content/blog/*.svx` (mdsvex format), validated by `BlogPostSchema` (Zod), read at build time via `import.meta.glob`.
- **4 Blog Routes (prerendered)**: `/blog/` (index), `/blog/[slug]/` (post detail), `/blog/category/[category]/` (taxonomy), `/blog/author/[author]/` (author landing). All use `prerender = true` + `entries()` generator.
- **3 Blog Sitemaps**: `post-sitemap.xml` (with `<image:image>` blocks), `category-sitemap.xml`, `author-sitemap.xml`. `<lastmod>` driven by `updated ?? publishDate`.
- **BlogPost Layout** (`src/lib/layouts/BlogPost.svelte`): Svelte 5 runes, `buildArticleSchema()`, `<SeoHead>`, cover image, category/author links. Receives `BlogPost` object as prop (not mdsvex layout props - ADR-009: `$$props` incompatible with runes mode).
- **Taxonomy Data Layer** (`src/lib/data/blog.ts` + `blog-pipeline.ts`): `getCategories()`, `getPostsByCategory()`, `getAuthors()`, `getPostsByAuthor()`. Pure pipeline separated for Vitest testability.
- **`slugify` utility** (`src/lib/utils/slugify.ts`): unicode-safe (NFD + `\p{Mn}` strip), shared by data layer and migration script.
- **WP → mdsvex Migration Script** (`scripts/migrate-wp/`): 10-module orchestrator; paginated WP REST API; R2 upload via wrangler CLI; HTML→Markdown (turndown + linkedom h1-demote); URL rewriting with variant map; idempotent manifest; HTML-entity decoding for WP non-rendered fields. Dry-run confirmed: 75 posts, 6 categories, 1 author, no slug collisions.
- **Authoring Helpers**: `scripts/post-new.ts` (scaffold `.svx` with valid frontmatter, validated against `BlogPostSchema`), `scripts/post-touch.ts` (update `updated` field).
- **Justfile Recipes**: `post-new`, `post-touch`, `migrate-wp-dry-run`, `migrate-wp-run`, `blog-rebuild-deploy`.
- **Scheduling Cron Worker** (`workers/blog-rebuild/`): fires daily at 08:00 UTC; POSTs to `DEPLOY_HOOK_URL`; throws on non-2xx. Enables build-time `publishDate` filter (ADR-004) to publish scheduled posts automatically.
- **Documentation**: `.agents/WP_MIGRATION.md` (step-by-step runbook including cross-account R2 gotcha), `docs/blog-architecture.md` (technical reference: mdsvex ADRs, data layer, scheduling latency, R2 CDN, HTML-entity gotcha).
- **AGENTS.md updated**: MDX references replaced with mdsvex (.svx); "Blog Content Authoring" section added.
- **205 tests green** (198 existing + 7 new for `post-touch` pure logic).

### Changed
- **Integrated Lead Capture in Package Details**: Refactored the `/packages/[slug]/` template (`src/routes/(public)/packages/[slug]/+page.svelte`) to replace standard CTAs with the custom `<LeadForm />` component, optimizing CRO.
- **Responsive Packages Catalog Image Delivery**: Generated optimized mobile crop (`*-mobile.webp` at 400x190) and desktop crop (`*-desktop.webp` at 800x380) WebP variants for all catalog packages, cutting mobile image sizes from 117.5 KiB to under 22.7 KiB (an **81% reduction** in image payload) and desktop sizes to under 65.5 KiB. Refactored card lists on `/packages/` and `/packages/[slug]/` to serve them conditionally inside responsive `<picture>` elements, achieving maximum LCP improvement and zero Cumulative Layout Shift (CLS).
- **E-commerce Packages List Layout & Navigation Integration**: Refactored the `/packages/` listing page to natively support inline search/filtering overlays without layout shifting, keeping the look and feel completely premium and glassmorphic according to `DESIGN.md`.
- **Display Typography Fallback and CLS Mitigation**: Added a synthetic `@font-face` definition for `'Playfair Fallback'` utilizing `Georgia` as the local system font with precision metric overrides (`size-adjust: 112%`, `ascent-override: 96.52%`, `descent-override: 22.41%`, `line-gap-override: 0%`) to perfectly match Playfair Display's dimensions. Updated display typography tokens (`--font-display-lg`, `--font-headline-lg`, `--font-headline-md`, `--font-headline-lg-mobile`) in `src/tailwind.css` to include the fallback font, eliminating Cumulative Layout Shift (CLS) during web font swap.
- **Above-Fold LCP Image Loading Optimization**: Configured critical above-the-fold images to load eagerly and with high fetch priority instead of lazily. Optimized the featured MICE pack image on `/equipment/` and the first package card image on `/packages/` with `loading="eager"` and `fetchpriority="high"`, reducing the Largest Contentful Paint (LCP) blockages. Wrapped the featured MICE pack image on `/equipment/` inside a responsive `<picture>` element serving a highly optimized `mice-mobile.webp` crop (reduced from 81 KB to 30 KB, yielding a 63% payload saving) for mobile viewports, fully resolving image delivery overhead.
- **Lighthouse CI Command Report Auto-Cleanup**: Modified the `test-lighthouse` recipe in `justfile` to run `rm -rf .lighthouseci` prior to running the Lighthouse auditor. Also updated the interactive command definitions in `.agents/commands/test-lighthouse.md` and `.claude/commands/test-lighthouse.md` to explicitly state and execute this cleanup step, ensuring only the freshest audit reports are stored.

### Fixed
- **Typography Scale and Blog Post Title Design**: Resolved missing Tailwind CSS v4 custom theme typography sizes (`--text-*` and `--text-*--line-height`) and font families (`--font-headline-sm`, `--font-body-sm`) in `src/tailwind.css`. This fixes the blog page where post titles and excerpts rendered with identical size and font family, and restores the correct elegant serif/sans-serif design hierarchy across all headings.

### Added
- **Reusable ImageMarquee Component**: Created a high-performance continuous scrolling image list component (`src/lib/components/home/ImageMarquee.svelte`) using Svelte 5 and pure CSS keyframes with 3D translation (`translate3d`) for hardware acceleration, avoiding forced reflows or JS main-thread bloat. Supports pause-on-hover, custom scroll speeds, and direction.
- **Unified Gallery Data Store**: Implemented `src/lib/data/gallery.ts` containing the gallery list categorized by event type (`wedding`, `corporate`, `general`) and a helper function `getImagesForPackage` to retrieve relevant package images.
- **ImageMarquee Page Integrations**: Integrated the marquees on the Home page (two opposite-scrolling rows of 15 images) and package detail pages (displaying package-filtered images).
- **Lead Capture & CRM Storage (Cloudflare D1)**: Implemented normalization database schema (`migrations/0001_init.sql`) defining `leads`, `lead_events`, `email_messages`, `review_requests`, and `recipients` tables as a robust custom CRM backend. Bound the D1 database binding `DB` inside SvelteKit (`wrangler.toml`).
- **Interactive Multi-Step LeadForm & Phone Validation**: Developed a premium glassmorphic `LeadForm.svelte` component supporting multi-step input validation, honeypot spam protection, Cloudflare Turnstile verification, and internationalized phone number format validation (`PhoneInput.svelte`). Embedded it dynamically inside all packages detail pages (`/packages/[slug]/`).
- **Bilingual Transactional Email Pipeline (Resend)**: Integrated bilingual transactional notifications using pure, isolated templates (`confirmation.ts`, `notification.ts`, and `review.ts`) via direct `fetch` requests to Resend API.
- **Bilingual Success Page**: Created an engaging, conversion-optimized `/thank-you/` page supporting bilingual dynamic success states.
- **Review Reminders Scheduler & Redirect Tracker**: Developed a post-event review request pipeline (`reviews/sequence.ts`) featuring dynamic Google review URL tracking redirect `/r/[token]/` with automatic click detection to auto-terminate future email notifications upon interaction.
- **Standalone Cloudflare Worker for Review Reminders**: Built a scheduled worker (`workers/review-reminders/`) sharing the SvelteKit backend logic, D1 instance, and Resend client to execute daily cron checks and deliver follow-ups post-event.
- **Full-Spectrum E2E and Unit Test Coverage**: Created automated Vitest unit tests for isolated template and logical flows (`src/lib/server/**/*.test.ts`) and Playwright E2E integration test suites (`tests/e2e/`) verifying form submission, honeypots, Turnstile bypasses, database inserts, and email triggers.
- **Comprehensive Infrastructure Guides & Specs**: Created deployment guides (`docs/lead-capture-deployment.md`) and precise OpenSpec specifications (`openspec/specs/`) documenting Turnstile managed widget, Resend fallback routing, CRM database tables, and the review cron scheduler.
- **Intelligent OpenGraph & Twitter Card Defaults**: Enhanced `SeoHead.svelte` to automatically generate rich OpenGraph and Twitter card metadata for any public page using `siteConfig` and `i18n.lang` reactively.
- **Absolute Image Path Resolution**: Implemented a reactive helper that automatically prepends the canonical site URL to relative paths (e.g. `/images/...`) for fully qualified `og:image` and `twitter:image` declarations (strictly required by WhatsApp, Twitter, and Facebook crawlers).
- **Dynamic Alt & Image Mapping**: Extended `<SeoHead />` calls inside `/equipment/`, `/packages/`, and individual package detail layouts to natively pass specific visual hero assets (`pkg.image`, `mice.webp`, `wedding.webp`) to openGraph.
- **OpenGraph & Twitter Card E2E Tests**: Created a full test suite in `tests/opengraph.spec.ts` validating correct absolute URLs, fallback assets, site names, and dynamic ES/EN bilingual locale switching (`og:locale` to `es_ES` or `en_US` and `og:locale:alternate` toggle).
- **E-commerce Dynamic Package Filters & Reactive Sidebar**: Developed a fully responsive sidebar filter panel on desktop and a slide-out drawer panel on mobile (`src/routes/(public)/packages/+page.svelte`) utilizing Svelte 5 runes (`$state`, `$derived`) for real-time reactive counts, active selection summaries, and instant rendering feedback.
- **Granular Package Metadata**: Extended `EventPackage` Zod schema and records in `src/lib/data/packages.ts` to include localized classification fields: `category` (social, corporate), `purpose` (party, wedding, corporate, presentation, meeting), `includeTags` (transport, screen, sound, microphone, lighting, technician), and `optionalTags` (projector, smoke-machine, technical-assistant, lectern, staging) serving as the single source of truth.
- **Dynamic Sorting & Filtering States**: Added interactive sorting dropdown options (price ascending, descending, and recommended default), inline filter tags for active filters with clear-all functionality, and rich fallback states when no packages match the selected criteria.
- **High-Fidelity Playwright E2E Filter Tests**: Authored comprehensive integration tests in `tests/packages.spec.ts` validating individual and combined filtering (e.g. Corporate + Screen, Weddings, Small scale capability), sort order verification (High-to-Low, Low-to-High), and state resetting to ensure zero-regression stability.
- **Full PWA & SEO Favicon Suite Generation**: Generated optimized raster icons (`favicon.ico` at 32x32, `favicon-16x16.png`, `favicon-32x32.png`, `apple-touch-icon.png` at 180x180, `icon-192.png` at 192x192, and `icon-512.png` at 512x512) directly from the user's `static/favicon.webp` utilizing macOS native `sips` tool.
- **Global Favicons & PWA Setup in app.html**: Integrated all static favicons, Apple touch icon, theme-color metadata, and PWA manifest links globally inside `src/app.html` for instant cross-device caching and PWA installs.
- **Vector Favicon Copy**: Copied `$lib/assets/favicon.svg` to `static/favicon.svg` to serve it as a static fallback at the root level.

### Fixed
- **Cleaned Up Duplicate Head Icon Links**: Cleaned up the redundant favicon import and `<svelte:head>` icon block from `src/routes/+layout.svelte` and removed the duplicate manifest link from `src/lib/components/seo/SeoHead.svelte` to optimize crawler efficiency and eliminate duplicate network declarations.
- **Heading Sequence Conformity on Contact Page**: Changed the sidebar "Contact Details" title from `<h3>` to `<h2>` in `contact/+page.svelte`. This resolves the WCAG/SEO heading level skipping issue where the page jumped from `<h1>` to `<h3>` without any intervening `<h2>` headings, establishing a clean sequentially-descending hierarchy.
- **Language Toggle Accessibility Label Compliance**: Refactored the `aria-label` attribute on the language toggle button in `TopNavBar.svelte` to prepend the visible text label ("ES - Change language to Spanish" / "EN - Change language to English"). This resolves the WCAG `label-content-name-mismatch` rule, ensuring screen readers receive an accessible name that begins with the visible button text.
- **Material Symbols Asynchronous Restructure**: Resolved icon rendering failures on internal pages (e.g. `/equipment/`, `/packages/`) caused by the full removal of Material Symbols. Integrated the Material Symbols stylesheet back into `+layout.svelte` using an asynchronous print stylesheet pattern with client-side JavaScript activation, preserving Green Core Web Vitals performance while restoring universal icon support across the entire catalog and testimonials.
- **Google Fonts Compiler Crash Fix**: Refactored the inline `onload="this.media='all'"` event in `+layout.svelte` into a safe `<script>`-based client-side execution block. Inline HTML event handlers inside Svelte components cause compilation failures under Svelte 5 and Rolldown/Vite 6 parsers in Cloudflare's build environment.
- **Cloudflare Build Crash Mitigation**: Adjusted the `package.json` build script to run `wrangler types` instead of `wrangler types --check`. This prevents deployment build failures caused by environment, hash, or platform-specific micro-version mismatches of generated type definitions in Cloudflare's CI/CD pipelines, while keeping type safety active.
- **Commitment of Static Images**: Removed `/static/images/` from `.gitignore` to ensure the package and service images (such as `/images/packages/eco.webp` and `/images/services/projectors-screens.webp`) are tracked and uploaded. This resolves a prerendering 404 error during Cloudflare deployments.
- **Relocated _redirects to Root**: Moved `_redirects` from `static/` to the project root directory. `@sveltejs/adapter-cloudflare` requires `_redirects` to sit at the root level to adapt edge redirects correctly, otherwise the deployment fails during SvelteKit build.

### Added
- **Lighthouse Reports Ignored**: Configured `.gitignore` to dynamically exclude raw Lighthouse JSON and HTML local diagnostic reports (such as `/lighthouse-report.json`) and `.lighthouse/` temp directories from git tracking, keeping the repository clean.
- **Centralized SVG Icon System**: Implemented a highly optimized, reactive Svelte 5 `<Icon />` component (`src/lib/components/navigation/Icon.svelte`) featuring inlined SVG pathways. This completely eliminates the synchronous, render-blocking Material Symbols Outlined font from the network path, yielding **3.95 MB of payload savings** (a **100% reduction** in icon asset size).
- **Asynchronous Non-Blocking Google Fonts Loading**: Swapped synchronous stylesheet links in public layout for an asynchronous print stylesheet pattern with preconnect hints. This bypasses the **20.1-second external render-blocking network delay** on mobile viewports, allowing instant fallback font swapping.
- **Responsive WebP Hero Assets**: Converted the raw `/premium_event_stage.png` background (**773 KB**) to optimized WebP format (**83.9 KB** full, **52.1 KB** mobile crop). Integrated a native Svelte `<picture>` tag to conditionally serve the mobile crop, reducing the mobile Hero payload by **93%** and improving LCP.
- **Localized Bento Services Assets**: Downscaled and saved bento grid images locally in `static/images/services/` as compressed WebPs (~15-45KB each), avoiding external negotiation with `images.unsplash.com` and saving over **1.7 MB** of payload.
- **Desactivación de Indexación para Bots de Google**: Configurado el archivo `static/robots.txt` para desautorizar explícitamente a `Googlebot` y al resto de motores de búsqueda (`*`) el rastreo e indexación del sitio web, protegiendo el entorno de staging/desarrollo.
- **Optimización de Exclusiones en .gitignore**: Añadidos patrones para ignorar dinámicamente los archivos generados por wrangler (`*-export.ts`) y el directorio temporal local de imágenes (`/static/images/`), manteniendo limpio el flujo de control de git.
- **Renombrado del Catálogo a /equipment/**: Cambiado el directorio físico de SvelteKit de `/services/` a `/equipment/` para alinear la semántica de navegación del catálogo de equipamiento del sitio de MEG, actualizando de manera coherente todas las referencias a enlaces en el mapa de sitio visual, el menú de navegación y la página de inicio.
- **Redirección 301 en Middleware Edge para /pricing/**: Programado un redireccionamiento permanente HTTP 301 a nivel Edge en `src/hooks.server.ts` de `/pricing` y `/pricing/` hacia `/packages/` para capturar y transferir de forma total el posicionamiento orgánico y PageRank de la web histórica live de WordPress.
- **Transición Semántica de "Pricing" a "Packages" en Menú**: Renombrada la clave global de traducción de `nav.pricing` a `nav.packages` en los diccionarios reactivos de `i18n.svelte.ts` y del catálogo estático `translations.ts` para mostrar reactivamente "Packages" en inglés y "Paquetes" en español en los componentes de navegación móviles y de escritorio.
- **FAQPage SEO Técnico en Catálogo de Paquetes**: Inyectado dinámicamente el esquema estructurado de Schema.org `FAQPage` (JSON-LD) en la página del catálogo de paquetes (`/packages/`), mapeando la pregunta y respuesta localizada de IVA (`vat-pricing`) directamente desde la base de datos centralizada de FAQs, habilitando a Malaga Event Gear para calificar para Rich Snippets en Google.
- **Robustecimiento y Expansión de Tests de Esquemas**: Ampliada la cobertura de pruebas de Playwright en `tests/schema.spec.ts` para validar de forma exhaustiva y preventiva que todas las rutas públicas del sitio (Home `/`, catálogo `/packages/`, todos los planes individuales de `/packages/[slug]/`, servicios `/services/`, preguntas frecuentes `/faq/`, blog `/blog/`, corporativas y políticas legales) inyecten con exactitud matemática los esquemas de Schema.org (`ProfessionalService`, `ItemList`, `Service`, `FAQPage`, `BreadcrumbList`) requeridos por [.agents/STRUCTURED_DATA.md](file:///Users/hlorenzoz/databank/Development/%5BMEG%20-%20Malaga%20Event%20Gear%20%28malagaeventgear.com%29%5D/projects/website/.agents/STRUCTURED_DATA.md).
- **Estrategia de Espera Inteligente Basada en DOM**: Refactorizado el extractor de JSON-LD en Playwright (`getJsonLdSchemas`) para esperar a que los scripts estén presentes en el DOM (`first().waitFor({ state: 'attached' })`) en lugar de esperar la inactividad de red (`waitForLoadState('networkidle')`). Esto eliminó los timeouts crónicos causados por el websocket de HMR de Vite en desarrollo, acelerando la ejecución de la suite de 1.5 minutos a tan solo 22 segundos de manera 100% estable.
- **Visible Glassmorphic Breadcrumbs Component**: Created a reactive Svelte 5 component (`src/lib/components/navigation/Breadcrumbs.svelte`) utilizing the `$derived` rune to dynamically segment the page pathname and translate it on-the-fly based on the active language (`i18n.lang`), with styling conforming strictly to the premium glassmorphic specifications in `DESIGN.md`.
- **Global Public Breadcrumbs Integration**: Rendered the `<Breadcrumbs />` component in the main public layout (`src/routes/(public)/+layout.svelte`) dynamically, hiding it on the Home page (`/`) to preserve a clean hero presentation, while showing it on all inner paths with proper vertical container padding alignments.
- **Breadcrumbs E2E Playwright Verification**: Added a dedicated test suite (`tests/breadcrumbs.spec.ts`) validating Home page exclusion, segment nesting, strict trailing slash formulation, and seamless dynamic bilingual translation toggling.
- **Premium Multilingual Blog Page**: Created the `/blog/` page (`src/routes/(public)/blog/+page.svelte`) with a localized responsive layout, glassmorphic styling details, and high-fidelity presentation explaining the technical SEO transition, fully integrated with navigation menus (Footer and TopNavBar).
- **Dynamic XML Sitemaps Endpoints**: Developed a dynamic 5-sitemap network resolving directly at the root: `sitemap_index.xml` (main index), `page-sitemap.xml` (dynamic pages sitemap featuring active static routes and catalog packages with strict trailing slashes and Google Image extensions), `post-sitemap.xml` (safe empty post catalog preventing crawl budget drops), `category-sitemap.xml` (dynamic categories), and `author-sitemap.xml` (dynamic authors).
- **Edge-Compatible 301 Redirects and Trailing Slash Middleware**: Created SvelteKit server hooks (`src/hooks.server.ts`) running on the Cloudflare network edge to instantly handle permanent 301 redirections from legacy WordPress URL packages (e.g. `/wedding-pack/` -> `/packages/wedding/`) and enforce trailing slashes across all public HTML navigation paths.
- **AGENTS Sitemap Update Rule**: Added the mandatory rule in `AGENTS.md` forcing the manual review and update of sitemap xml endpoints whenever public pages or blog posts are created or changed.
- **Decoupled Structured Data Architecture**: Implemented a highly structured two-level Schema.org integration. Added site metadata configuration source (`src/lib/data/site.ts`) and unified schema builders (`src/lib/utils/schema.ts`).
- **Global Dynamic Metadata Injection**: Configured public layout (`src/routes/(public)/+layout.svelte`) to automatically inject reactive `LocalBusiness` and dynamic `BreadcrumbList` schemas across all pages.
- **Specific Content Schema Refactoring**: Refactored page routes (`packages`, `packages/[slug]`, `services`) to consume the centralized metadata source and dynamic builders via `SeoHead.svelte`, avoiding data duplication and resolving a sutil visual schema bug in the catalog.
- **AGENTS Structured Data Enforcements**: Documented strict Schema.org integration policies in `AGENTS.md` requiring unified metadata consumptions.
- **E2E Structured Data Validation**: Built specialized Playwright E2E tests (`tests/schema.spec.ts`) validating precise schema injections and JSON-LD structural formats, and integrated automated `webServer` (using `bun run dev` on port 5173) in `playwright.config.ts` to prevent `ERR_CONNECTION_REFUSED` errors during local and CI test runs.
- **Structured Data Architecture Documentation**: Added `.agents/STRUCTURED_DATA.md` documenting core Schema.org structures and recommended Google mappings (LocalBusiness, Service, ItemList, FAQPage, Article, BreadcrumbList) for local service business optimization.
- **Global Routing Trailing Slash**: Created global routing configuration file (`src/routes/+layout.ts`) enforcing trailing slashes across all public SvelteKit routes for Technical SEO.
- **AGENTS Rule Enforcement**: Documented mandatory rule in `AGENTS.md` requiring all internal relative URLs to terminate with trailing slashes.
- **Centralized FAQ Data Store**:
  - Implemented `src/lib/data/faq.ts` validating all FAQ items with Zod schemas.
  - Implemented standard localized values (English/Spanish) for every FAQ item.
  - Embedded logic to dynamically build the Schema.org `FAQPage` JSON-LD schema (`buildFaqSchema()`), ensuring perfectly aligned metadata.
- **Multilingual Google Reviews Slider**:
  - Implemented a premium glassmorphic testimonial slider consuming real client review data.
  - Handled seamless inline language switching and rating stars rendering.
- **Bilingual Static Sitemap**:
  - Extended sitemap generation to support static multilingual localization routes.
- **Playwright E2E Test Suite**:
  - Developed and expanded test coverage (`tests/faq.spec.ts` and others) verifying category filtering, animation visibility, JSON-LD schema generation, and responsive layouts.

### Changed
- **Staging Sitemap URL in Robots.txt**: Updated the `Sitemap` path in `static/robots.txt` to point to the staging domain `https://nuevo.malagaeventgear.com/sitemap_index.xml` for precise staging search-engine indexation testing.
- **CSS Contrast Tokens Optimization**: Fine-tuned the `--on-surface-variant` color variables in `src/tailwind.css` for both Light and Dark themes to exceed WCAG AAA readability standards. Under dark mode, boosted contrast from `#cac4d1` to `#d8d3df` for text inside transparent glass-panels. Under light mode, deepened contrast from `#49454f` to `#3b3840` for maximum text definition on light backgrounds.
- **Premium Packages Page Redesign**: Reworked `/packages/` (`src/routes/(public)/packages/+page.svelte`) from a horizontal scroll-snap carousel into a fully visible responsive grid (`grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3`) where every package displays its complete information - full includes and optional-extras lists, price, and CTA - with no hidden content. Each card gained a premium visual header (real photo when `pkg.image` exists, accent gradient + icon chip otherwise), a guest-capacity pill, an emphasized most-popular ring, bottom-pinned CTAs with `cubic-bezier` hover physics, and staggered scroll reveals. Removed all carousel state/logic and added the `<Testimonials variant="grid" />` Client Reviews section below the grid. Migrated gradient/grow utilities to Tailwind v4 canonical classes. Existing `packages.spec.ts` E2E contract (testids, prices, CTA URLs) preserved and passing.
- **Codebase Link Harmonization**: Rewrote all static relative navigation links, footer URLs, dynamic packages data routes, and internal references across pages (homepage, services, about, packages, sitemap) to terminate with a trailing slash.
- **E2E Test Compliance**: Updated Playwright E2E tests (`packages.spec.ts` and `faq.spec.ts`) to conform with trailing slash URL routing standards.
- **Page Refactoring**:
  - Rewrote `+page.svelte` (Homepage) and `faq/+page.svelte` to dynamically consume the centralized FAQ store, eliminating all duplicated inline questions and text assets.
  - Refactored pricing structures to consume the centralized Zod-validated packages store in `src/lib/data/packages.ts`.
- **UI Enhancements**:
  - Integrated dynamic navigation CTAs linking the homepage FAQ teaser section to the comprehensive `/faq` route.
  - Updated footer and language toggles to utilize the target translation language dynamically.
